no_change
Player: I watch the bridge for hidden watchers.
Narration: Your keen eyes sweep across the jagged contours of the rock shelf overlooking the quarry road. You find nothing immediately obvious—no figures huddled in shadow, no tell-tale glint of a hidden scope. However, near a cluster of mossy boulders, you catch a fleeting glimpse of movement. A lone figure, draped in dark cloth matching the Sable Chain's usual livery, shifts subtly behind the shelf edge, seemingly watching the main thoroughfare with intense focus. This watcher does not move or acknowledge your presence yet, but their posture suggests vigilance and readiness to react instantly.
